Expected: PASS.

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the handler and register the route**

Append to `internal/web/api_projects.go`:

```go
// handleDeleteGame deletes a game outright. Owner-only, like
// handleChangeRole and unlike self-removal from handleRemoveMember:
// deleting the game is not a member's own choice the way leaving it is,
// and its irreversibility (every membership, token and bound invite
// scoped to it disappears with it — see projects.Delete's own doc
// comment) is a different order of consequence than one person's own
// membership row. Maps projects.ErrLastOwner the same 409 way
// handleChangeRole and handleRemoveMember already do, defensively: a
// project always has at least one owner by construction (Create grants
// one atomically, and the last-owner guard refuses to let it drop to
// zero any other way), so this branch is unreachable today — but it
// costs nothing to keep mapped, and a future refactor that makes it
// reachable gets a 409 instead of silently falling through to a 500.
func (s *Server) handleDeleteGame(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, caller Caller, scope ProjectScope) {
	if !requireHumanCaller(w, caller) {
		return
	}
	if !roles.AtLeast(roles.Role(scope.Role), roles.Owner) {
		writeError(w, http.StatusForbidden, errCodeForbidden, "only an owner may delete a game")
		return
	}
	switch err := s.opts.Projects.Delete(r.Context(), scope.ProjectID); {
	case errors.Is(err, projects.ErrLastOwner):
		writeError(w, http.StatusConflict, errCodeLastOwner, "a game must keep at least one owner — promote someone else first")
	case err != nil:
		slog.ErrorContext(r.Context(), "delete game failed", "project_id", scope.ProjectID, "error", err)
		writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, errCodeInternal, "could not delete the game")
	default:
		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNoContent)
	}
}
```

Add to `NewServer` in `internal/web/server.go`, alongside Task 12's other
game routes:

```go
	s.mux.Handle("DELETE /api/games/{game}", requireCaller(s.requireProject(s.handleDeleteGame)))
```

**This handler post-dates a Task 12 quality review that reshaped the
vocabulary it must be written against** — read that review's corrections
in Task 12's own section before implementing this step, not just this
snippet: `requireProjectAccess` no longer exists (replaced by the
`requireProject` wrapper and `ProjectScope`, handed to the handler as a
parameter instead of fetched by a call each handler had to remember to
make), `requireHumanCaller` is a package-level function, not a method,
and the bare `"forbidden"`/`"last_owner"`/`"internal_error"` string
literals shown in earlier drafts of this section are now the
`errCode*` constants in `auth.go`. The snippet above already reflects
all three; a reader implementing this task from an older copy of this
plan would not.

- **Covered here:** the binary and its four surfaces (Tasks 1, 10-15); config including registration modes and allowed email domains (Task 2); Postgres, embedded migrations, ephemeral test databases (Task 3); argon2id, sessions with the cookie flags the spec names, rate limiting, hashed invite and API tokens shown once (Tasks 4-9, 11, 12); projects, memberships, single-game navigation (Tasks 8, 12); MCP `whoami`/`games.*` with the game-isolation invariant tested for admins too (Task 13); SSE (Task 14); Docker, CI, the `make check` gate (Task 16).
- **Deliberately deferred to the metamodel plan:** entity types, entities, relation types, relations, field schemas and their validator, bulk upserts with `atomic`/`partial`, `expected_version` on domain writes, the `schema_violation` / `endpoint_type_mismatch` / `in_use` error shapes, search, and the `entity.*` / `relation.*` SSE event kinds. The Core plan lands the `Event` shape they will publish through.
- **Deferred to their own sub-projects:** the docs site generator and `make docs-check` (item 7 of the roadmap ships the agent skill bundle alongside it), `pg_dump` backups, and the real visual identity. `make check` in Task 16 omits `docs-check` because `cmd/maestro-docs` does not exist yet; add it in the same commit that creates the generator.
